Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a electrical_engineering algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 53. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 590/3.
